=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given a positive integer N, and an integer A_{x,y,z} for each triple of integers (x, y, z) such that 1 \leq x, y, z \leq N.
You will be given Q queries in the following format, which must be processed in order.
For the i-th query (1 \leq i \leq Q), you are given a tuple of integers (Lx_i, Rx_i, Ly_i, Ry_i, Lz_i, Rz_i) such that 1 \leq Lx_i \leq Rx_i \leq N, 1 \leq Ly_i \leq Ry_i \leq N, and 1 \leq Lz_i \leq Rz_i \leq N. Find:
\displaystyle{\sum_{x=Lx_i}^{Rx_i} \sum_{y=Ly_i}^{Ry_i} \sum_{z=Lz_i}^{Rz_i} A_{x,y,z}}.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N
A_{1,1,1} A_{1,1,2} \ldots A_{1,1,N}
A_{1,2,1} A_{1,2,2} \ldots A_{1,2,N}
\vdots
A_{1,N,1} A_{1,N,2} \ldots A_{1,N,N}
A_{2,1,1} A_{2,1,2} \ldots A_{2,1,N}
A_{2,2,1} A_{2,2,2} \ldots A_{2,2,N}
\vdots
A_{2,N,1} A_{2,N,2} \ldots A_{2,N,N}
\vdots
A_{N,1,1} A_{N,1,2} \ldots A_{N,1,N}
A_{N,2,1} A_{N,2,2} \ldots A_{N,2,N}
\vdots
A_{N,N,1} A_{N,N,2} \ldots A_{N,N,N}
Q
Lx_1 Rx_1 Ly_1 Ry_1 Lz_1 Rz_1
Lx_2 Rx_2 Ly_2 Ry_2 Lz_2 Rz_2
\vdots
Lx_Q Rx_Q Ly_Q Ry_Q Lz_Q Rz_Q

Output

Print Q lines.
The i-th line should contain the answer to the i-th query.

Constraints


- 1 \leq N \leq 100
- 1 \leq Q \leq 2 \times 10^{5}
- 0 \leq A_{x,y,z} \leq 999 (1 \leq x, y, z \leq N)
- 1 \leq Lx_i \leq Rx_i \leq N (1 \leq i \leq Q)
- 1 \leq Ly_i \leq Ry_i \leq N (1 \leq i \leq Q)
- 1 \leq Lz_i \leq Rz_i \leq N (1 \leq i \leq Q)
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

2
1 2
3 4
5 6
7 8
2
1 2 2 2 1 1
2 2 1 2 1 2

Sample Output 1

10
26

For the 1st query, the sought value is A_{1,2,1} + A_{2,2,1} = 3 + 7 = 10. Thus, print 10.
For the 2nd query, the sought value is A_{2,1,1} + A_{2,1,2} + A_{2,2,1} + A_{2,2,2} = 5 + 6 + 7 + 8 = 26. Thus, print 26.

306 942 694

8
3 3 3 3 1 1
1 3 2 3 3 3
2 2 2 3 1 1
1 3 1 1 1 1
2 3 2 3 2 3
1 2 1 1 1 2
3 3 2 2 1 3
1 2 2 3 2 3

Sample Output 2

3917
551

5180
3311
1010

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

```
def main():
    import sys
    data = sys.stdin.buffer.read()
    idx = 0
    L = len(data)
    def ni():
        nonlocal idx
        while idx < L and data[idx] <= 32:
            idx += 1
        num = 0
        while idx < L and data[idx] > 32:
            num = num * 10 + (data[idx] - 48)
            idx += 1
        return num

    N = ni()
    M = N + 1
    stride_y = M
    stride_x = M * M
    size = stride_x * M
    S = [0] * size

    for x in range(1, N+1):
        base_x = x * stride_x
        for y in range(1, N+1):
            idx0 = base_x + y * stride_y
            for z in range(1, N+1):
                S[idx0 + z] = ni()
```
This fills. Then prefix:
```
    for x in range(1, N+1):
        base_x = x * stride_x
        base_xm = base_x - stride_x
        for y in range(1, N+1):
            idx = base_x + y * stride_y
            idx_xm = base_xm + y * stride_y
            idx_ym = base_x + (y - 1) * stride_y
            idx_xm_ym = base_xm + (y - 1) * stride_y
            # z=1..N

                    S[idx_xm + z] + S[idx_ym + z] + S[idx + z - 1]
```

```
    Q = ni()
    out = []

        lx = ni(); rx = ni(); ly = ni(); ry = ni(); lz = ni(); rz = ni()
        # compute

        x1 = rx * stride_x

        y1 = ry * stride_y

        ans = (S[x1 + y1 + z1]

               - S[x1 + y0 + z1]
               - S[x1 + y1 + z0]
```

```
for x in range(1, N+1):
    for y in range(1, N+1):
        base = x*SX + y*SY

            idx = base + z
            val = ni()

```

Let's write final code with combined prefix and custom parser. Need decide variable names.

```
import sys

def main():
    data = sys.stdin.buffer.read()
    idx = 0
    L = len(data)

    def ni():
        nonlocal idx
        while idx < L and data[idx] <= 32:
            idx += 1
        num = 0
        while idx < L and data[idx] > 32:
            num = num * 10 + data[idx] - 48
            idx += 1
        return num

    N = ni()
    M = N + 1
    SY = M
    SX = M * M
    S = [0] * (SX * M)

    for x in range(1, N + 1):
        for y in range(1, N + 1):
            base = x * SX + y * SY
            for z in range(1, N + 1):
                idx_pos = base + z
                v = ni()
                S[idx_pos] = (
                    v
                    + S[idx_pos - SX]
                    + S[idx_pos - SY]
                    + S[idx_pos - 1]
                    - S[idx_pos - SX - SY]
                    - S[idx_pos - SX - 1]
                    - S[idx_pos - SY - 1]
                    + S[idx_pos - SX - SY - 1]
                )

    Q = ni()
    out = []
    append = out.append
    for _ in range(Q):
        lx = ni(); rx = ni(); ly = ni(); ry = ni(); lz = ni(); rz = ni()
        x0 = (lx - 1) * SX
        x1 = rx * SX
        y0 = (ly - 1) * SY
        y1 = ry * SY
        z0 = lz - 1
        z1 = rz
        ans = (
            S[x1 + y1 + z1]
            - S[x0 + y1 + z1]
            - S[x1 + y0 + z1]
            - S[x1 + y1 + z0]
            + S[x0 + y0 + z1]
            + S[x0 + y1 + z0]
            + S[x1 + y0 + z0]
            - S[x0 + y0 + z0]
        )
        append(str(ans))
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(out))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
Need check `idx_pos - SX - SY - 1` for x=1,y=1,z=1: idx_pos = SX + SY + 1; subtract SX+SY+1 =0. Good. For x=1,y=1,z=N: idx_pos = SX+SY+N; subtract SX+SY+1 = N-1? Wait S[x-1][y-1][z-1] = S[0][0][N-1] index = N-1. Formula idx - SX - SY -1 = (SX+SY+N) - SX - SY -1 = N-1. Yes. Good.

Now, final code with chunked output:
```
import sys

def main():
    data = sys.stdin.buffer.read()
    idx = 0
    L = len(data)

    def ni():
        nonlocal idx
        while idx < L and data[idx] <= 32:
            idx += 1
        num = 0
        while idx < L and data[idx] > 32:
            num = num * 10 + data[idx] - 48
            idx += 1
        return num

    N = ni()
    M = N + 1
    SY = M
    SX = M * M
    S = [0] * (SX * M)

    for x in range(1, N + 1):
        for y in range(1, N + 1):
            base = x * SX + y * SY
            for z in range(1, N + 1):
                p = base + z
                v = ni()
                S[p] = (
                    v
                    + S[p - SX]
                    + S[p - SY]
                    + S[p - 1]
                    - S[p - SX - SY]
                    - S[p - SX - 1]
                    - S[p - SY - 1]
                    + S[p - SX - SY - 1]
                )

    Q = ni()
    out = []
    write = sys.stdout.write
    CHUNK = 10000
    for _ in range(Q):
        lx = ni(); rx = ni(); ly = ni(); ry = ni(); lz = ni(); rz = ni()
        x0 = (lx - 1) * SX
        x1 = rx * SX
        y0 = (ly - 1) * SY
        y1 = ry * SY
        z0 = lz - 1
        z1 = rz
        ans = (
            S[x1 + y1 + z1]
            - S[x0 + y1 + z1]
            - S[x1 + y0 + z1]
            - S[x1 + y1 + z0]
            + S[x0 + y0 + z1]
            + S[x0 + y1 + z0]
            + S[x1 + y0 + z0]
            - S[x0 + y0 + z0]
        )
        out.append(str(ans))
        if len(out) == CHUNK:
            write("\n".join(out))
            write("\n")
            out.clear()
    if out:
        write("\n".join(out))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Inclusion-exclusion for 3D prefix:
P[x][y][z] = A[x][y][z]
+ P[x-1][y][z]
+ P[x][y-1][z]
+ P[x][y][z-1]
- P[x-1][y-1][z]
- P[x-1][y][z-1]
- P[x][y-1][z-1]
+ P[x-1][y-1][z-1]

Now index for P[x-1][y-1][z] = (x-1)*SX + (y-1)*SY + z = x*SX + y*SY + z - SX - SY = p - SX - SY. Yes.
P[x-1][y][z-1] = (x-1)*SX + y*SY + (z-1) = p - SX - 1. Yes.
P[x][y-1][z-1] = x*SX + (y-1)*SY + (z-1) = p - SY - 1. Yes.
P[x-1][y-1][z-1] = p - SX - SY - 1. Yes.
So formula correct.

Let's write final code carefully:

```
import sys

def main():
    data = sys.stdin.buffer.read() + b' '
    idx = 0

    def ni(data=data):
        nonlocal idx
        while data[idx] <= 32:
            idx += 1
        num = 0
        while data[idx] > 32:
            num = num * 10 + data[idx] - 48
            idx += 1
        return num

    N = ni()
    M = N + 1
    SY = M
    SX = M * M
    S = [0] * (SX * M)

    for x in range(1, N + 1):
        for y in range(1, N + 1):
            base = x * SX + y * SY
            for z in range(1, N + 1):
                p = base + z
                v = ni()
                S[p] = (
                    v
                    + S[p - SX]
                    + S[p - SY]
                    + S[p - 1]
                    - S[p - SX - SY]
                    - S[p - SX - 1]
                    - S[p - SY - 1]
                    + S[p - SX - SY - 1]
                )

    Q = ni()
    out = []
    write = sys.stdout.write
    CHUNK = 10000

    for _ in range(Q):
        lx = ni()
        rx = ni()
        ly = ni()
        ry = ni()
        lz = ni()
        rz = ni()

        x0 = (lx - 1) * SX
        x1 = rx * SX
        y0 = (ly - 1) * SY
        y1 = ry * SY
        z0 = lz - 1
        z1 = rz

        ans = (
            S[x1 + y1 + z1]
            - S[x0 + y1 + z1]
            - S[x1 + y0 + z1]
            - S[x1 + y1 + z0]
            + S[x0 + y0 + z1]
            + S[x0 + y1 + z0]
            + S[x1 + y0 + z0]
            - S[x0 + y0 + z0]
        )

        out.append(str(ans))
        if len(out) == CHUNK:
            write("\n".join(out))
            write("\n")
            out.clear()

    if out:
        write("\n".join(out))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's double-check syntax of `def ni(data=data):` with `nonlocal idx` inside. Is it allowed to have a default argument named `data` and then `nonlocal idx`? Yes. But note `data` is a parameter, so `data` is local; `nonlocal idx` doesn't affect `data`. Good.

Potential issue: In `ni`, `while data[idx] <= 32:` if `idx` is at len(data) because no token and we call ni? no. But what about after reading the last number, `idx` points to the appended space? Actually after reading last number, digit loop stops when `data[idx] > 32` false. If the last number is immediately before appended space (no trailing whitespace in original), after reading last digit, idx increments to the appended space index, digit loop sees data[idx]=32, stops. So idx points to appended space. If no further call, okay. If there is a trailing newline before appended space, digit loop stops at newline, idx points to newline. No further call. Good.

Potential issue: If input has trailing spaces and we append space, after reading last number, idx may point to a trailing space before appended space. No further call. Good.

Now, let's think about if `data[idx] <= 32` skip loop could skip the appended space and then index out of range if there is a call after all tokens. We don't. But what about if Q is read incorrectly due to parser bug? no.

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def main():
    data = sys.stdin.buffer.read() + b' '
    idx = 0

    def ni(data=data):
        nonlocal idx
        while data[idx] <= 32:
            idx += 1
        num = 0
        while data[idx] > 32:
            num = num * 10 + (data[idx] - 48)
            idx += 1
        return num

    N = ni()
    M = N + 1
    SY = M
    SX = M * M
    S = [0] * (SX * M)

    for x in range(1, N + 1):
        for y in range(1, N + 1):
            base = x * SX + y * SY
            for z in range(1, N + 1):
                p = base + z
                v = ni()
                S[p] = (
                    v
                    + S[p - SX]
                    + S[p - SY]
                    + S[p - 1]
                    - S[p - SX - SY]
                    - S[p - SX - 1]
                    - S[p - SY - 1]
                    + S[p - SX - SY - 1]
                )

    Q = ni()
    out = []
    write = sys.stdout.write
    CHUNK = 10000

    for _ in range(Q):
        lx = ni()
        rx = ni()
        ly = ni()
        ry = ni()
        lz = ni()
        rz = ni()

        x0 = (lx - 1) * SX
        x1 = rx * SX
        y0 = (ly - 1) * SY
        y1 = ry * SY
        z0 = lz - 1
        z1 = rz

        ans = (
            S[x1 + y1 + z1]
            - S[x0 + y1 + z1]
            - S[x1 + y0 + z1]
            - S[x1 + y1 + z0]
            + S[x0 + y0 + z1]
            + S[x0 + y1 + z0]
            + S[x1 + y0 + z0]
            - S[x0 + y0 + z0]
        )

        out.append(str(ans))
        if len(out) == CHUNK:
            write("\n".join(out))
            write("\n")
            out.clear()

    if out:
        write("\n".join(out))
        write("\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
